If the position instead *requires* more than this — the forced\n    ///        close-out band or the dust rule — the call reverts with `FullCloseRequired`\n    ///        rather than pulling more than authorised. Pass `type(uint256).max` to take the\n    ///        largest allowed bite. Call `previewLiquidation` first to size the approval.","originalFile":"// SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT\npragma solidity 0.8.28;\n\nimport {IERC20} from \"@openzeppelin/contracts/token/ERC20/IERC20.sol\";\nimport {IERC20Metadata} from \"@openzeppelin/contracts/token/ERC20/extensions/IERC20Metadata.sol\";\nimport {SafeERC20} from \"@openzeppelin/contracts/token/ERC20/utils/SafeERC20.sol\";\nimport {ReentrancyGuard} from \"@openzeppelin/contracts/utils/ReentrancyGuard.sol\";\nimport {Pausable} from \"@openzeppelin/contracts/utils/Pausable.sol\";\nimport {Ownable2Step, Ownable} from \"@openzeppelin/contracts/access/Ownable2Step.sol\";\nimport {Math} from \"@openzeppelin/contracts/utils/math/Math.sol\";\n\nimport {IPriceOracle} from \"./interfaces/IPriceOracle.sol\";\n\n/// @title LendingMarket\n/// @notice Single-pair overcollateralised borrowing market: lock WETH, borrow USDC.\n///\n/// Risk parameters (immutable, set at construction):\n///   - max LTV                 70%   — most you can borrow against your collateral\n///   - liquidation threshold   85%   — past this, the position can be liquidated\n///   - liquidation bonus        5%   — discount the liquidator gets on seized collateral\n///   - close factor            50%   — most of a debt that can be repaid in one liquidation,\n///                                     lifted to 100% once the position is underwater\n///\n/// Liquidity model: USDC lent out is supplied by the owner (`supply`/`withdrawSupply`).\n/// There is no public depositor side and therefore no lender shares — see NOTES.md.\n///\n/// Interest: a single flat annual rate applied to a global borrow index. Debt is stored as\n/// index-normalised shares, so per-borrower interest needs no per-borrower bookkeeping.\ncontract LendingMarket is Ownable2Step, Pausable, ReentrancyGuard {\n    using SafeERC20 for IERC20;\n\n    /* ---------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n    /*                                Constants                               */\n    /* ---------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n\n    /// @dev Basis points denominator. 10_000 bps == 100%.\n    uint256 public constant BPS = 10_000;\n\n    /// @dev Fixed-point one for the borrow index.\n    uint256 internal constant RAY = 1e27;\n\n    /// @dev Interest is quoted per this many seconds (365-day year, no leap-year handling).\n    uint256 internal constant SECONDS_PER_YEAR = 365 days;\n\n    /// @dev Ceiling on the borrow rate the owner may set, so the rate setter cannot be\n    ///      used to confiscate collateral by making debt explode.\n    uint256 public constant MAX_BORROW_RATE_BPS = 5_000; // 50% APR\n\n    /// @dev Health factor reported for a position with no debt.\n    uint256 public constant MAX_HEALTH_FACTOR = type(uint256).max;\n\n    /// @dev Health factor is scaled by 1e18. 1e18 == exactly at the liquidation threshold.\n    uint256 public constant HEALTH_FACTOR_SCALE = 1e18;\n\n    /* ---------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n    /*                          Immutable configuration                       */\n    /* ---------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n\n    /// @notice Collateral token (WETH on mainnet).\n    IERC20 public immutable collateralToken;\n\n    /// @notice Debt token (USDC on mainnet).\n    IERC20 public immutable debtToken;\n\n    /// @notice Price of 1 collateral token, quoted in debt-token units of account.\n    IPriceOracle public immutable oracle;\n\n    /// @dev 10 ** (collateralDecimals + priceDecimals - debtDecimals).\n    ///      Converts a raw collateral amount times a raw oracle price into a raw debt amount.\n    ///      Computed from the tokens' own `decimals()` — nothing here assumes 18.\n    uint256 public immutable priceScale;\n\n    /// @notice Maximum debt-to-collateral ratio a borrower may open or maintain, in bps.\n    uint256 public immutable maxLtvBps;\n\n    /// @notice Debt-to-collateral ratio past which a position may be liquidated, in bps.\n    uint256 public immutable liquidationThresholdBps;\n\n    /// @notice Extra collateral, in bps, a liquidator receives on top of what they repay.\n    uint256 public immutable liquidationBonusBps;\n\n    /// @notice Fraction of a debt, in bps, that a single liquidation may repay while the\n    ///         position still has more collateral value than debt.\n    uint256 public immutable closeFactorBps;\n\n    /* ---------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n    /*                             Mutable state                              */\n    /* ---------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n\n    /// @notice Flat annual borrow rate, in bps. Owner-settable within `MAX_BORROW_RATE_BPS`.\n    uint256 public borrowRateBps;\n\n    /// @notice Cumulative interest index, RAY-scaled. Starts at RAY and only grows.\n    uint256 public borrowIndex;\n\n    /// @notice Timestamp the index was last brought up to date.\n    uint256 public lastAccrualTimestamp;\n\n    /// @notice Sum of all borrowers' debt shares.\n    uint256 public totalDebtShares;\n\n    /// @notice Ceiling on total outstanding debt, in debt-token units. 0 disables new borrows.\n    uint256 public borrowCap;\n\n    /// @notice Ceiling on total collateral held, in collateral-token units.\n    uint256 public collateralCap;\n\n    /// @notice Smallest debt a position may carry. Positions must be at zero or at/above this,\n    ///         so that a liquidation is always worth more than the gas to perform it.\n    uint256 public minDebt;\n\n    /// @notice Total collateral currently held on behalf of borrowers (excludes donations).\n    uint256 public totalCollateral;\n\n    /// @notice Per-user collateral balance, in collateral-token units.\n    mapping(address account => uint256 amount) public collateralOf;\n\n    /// @notice Per-user debt shares. Multiply by `borrowIndex / RAY` for the debt owed.\n    mapping(address account => uint256 shares) public debtSharesOf;\n\n    /* ---------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n    /*                                 Events                                 */\n    /* ---------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n\n    event CollateralDeposited(address indexed account, uint256 amount);\n    event CollateralWithdrawn(address indexed account, address indexed to, uint256 amount);\n    event Borrowed(address indexed account, address indexed to, uint256 amount, uint256 shares);\n    event Repaid(address indexed payer, address indexed account, uint256 amount, uint256 shares);\n    event Liquidated(\n        address indexed liquidator,\n        address indexed account,\n        uint256 debtRepaid,\n        uint256 collateralSeized,\n        uint256 sharesBurned\n    );\n    event BadDebtRecorded(address indexed account, uint256 amount, uint256 shares);\n    event BadDebtWrittenOff(address indexed account, uint256 amount, uint256 shares);\n    event InterestAccrued(uint256 borrowIndex, uint256 totalDebt);\n    event BorrowRateSet(uint256 oldRateBps, uint256 newRateBps);\n    event BorrowCapSet(uint256 oldCap, uint256 newCap);\n    event CollateralCapSet(uint256 oldCap, uint256 newCap);\n    event MinDebtSet(uint256 oldMinDebt, uint256 newMinDebt);\n    event LiquiditySupplied(address indexed from, uint256 amount);\n    event LiquidityWithdrawn(address indexed to, uint256 amount);\n\n    /* ---------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n    /*                                 Errors                                 */\n    /* ---------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n\n    error ZeroAddress();\n    error ZeroAmount();\n    error InvalidParameters();\n    error InsufficientCollateral();\n    error InsufficientLiquidity(uint256 requested, uint256 available);\n    error BorrowCapExceeded(uint256 wouldBe, uint256 cap);\n    error CollateralCapExceeded(uint256 wouldBe, uint256 cap);\n    error PositionUnhealthy(uint256 healthFactor);\n    error PositionHealthy(uint256 healthFactor);\n    error DebtBelowMinimum(uint256 debt, uint256 minDebt);\n    error NoDebt();\n    error NothingToLiquidate();\n    error FullCloseRequired(uint256 required);\n    error SlippageExceeded(uint256 got, uint256 minWanted);\n    error PositionStillCollateralised(uint256 collateral);\n    error UnexpectedTokenBehaviour();\n\n    /* ---------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n    /*                              Construction                              */\n    /* ---------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n\n    struct Params {\n        address owner;\n        address collateralToken;\n        address debtToken;\n        address oracle;\n        uint256 maxLtvBps;\n        uint256 liquidationThresholdBps;\n        uint256 liquidationBonusBps;\n        uint256 closeFactorBps;\n        uint256 borrowRateBps;\n        uint256 borrowCap;\n        uint256 collateralCap;\n        uint256 minDebt;\n    }\n\n    constructor(Params memory p) Ownable(p.owner) {\n        if (p.collateralToken == address(0) || p.debtToken == address(0) || p.oracle == address(0)) {\n            revert ZeroAddress();\n        }\n        if (p.collateralToken == p.debtToken) revert InvalidParameters();\n\n        // LTV must sit strictly below the liquidation threshold, otherwise a borrower can\n        // open a position that is immediately liquidatable.\n        if (p.maxLtvBps == 0 || p.maxLtvBps >= p.liquidationThresholdBps) revert InvalidParameters();\n        // The threshold plus the bonus must stay under 100%, otherwise the first liquidation\n        // of a position at the threshold already seizes more collateral than the debt is worth.\n        if (p.liquidationThresholdBps >= BPS) revert InvalidParameters();\n        if (p.liquidationBonusBps == 0 || p.liquidationBonusBps > 2_000) revert InvalidParameters();\n        if (Math.mulDiv(p.liquidationThresholdBps, BPS + p.liquidationBonusBps, BPS) > BPS) {\n            revert InvalidParameters();\n        }\n        if (p.closeFactorBps == 0 || p.closeFactorBps > BPS) revert InvalidParameters();\n        if (p.borrowRateBps > MAX_BORROW_RATE_BPS) revert InvalidParameters();\n        if (p.minDebt == 0) revert InvalidParameters();\n\n        collateralToken = IERC20(p.collateralToken);\n        debtToken = IERC20(p.debtToken);\n        oracle = IPriceOracle(p.oracle);\n\n        maxLtvBps = p.maxLtvBps;\n        liquidationThresholdBps = p.liquidationThresholdBps;\n        liquidationBonusBps = p.liquidationBonusBps;\n        closeFactorBps = p.closeFactorBps;\n\n        borrowRateBps = p.borrowRateBps;\n        borrowCap = p.borrowCap;\n        collateralCap = p.collateralCap;\n        minDebt = p.minDebt;\n\n        borrowIndex = RAY;\n        lastAccrualTimestamp = block.timestamp;\n\n        // Derive the scaling factor from the live token/feed decimals rather than assuming\n        // 18/6/8. WETH(18) + ETHUSD(8) - USDC(6) => 1e20.\n        uint256 collDec = IERC20Metadata(p.collateralToken).decimals();\n        uint256 debtDec = IERC20Metadata(p.debtToken).decimals();\n        uint256 feedDec = IPriceOracle(p.oracle).priceDecimals();\n        if (collDec + feedDec < debtDec) revert InvalidParameters();\n        uint256 exponent = collDec + feedDec - debtDec;\n        if (exponent > 60) revert InvalidParameters();\n        priceScale = 10 ** exponent;\n    }\n\n    /* ---------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n    /*                          Interest accrual                              */\n    /* ---------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n\n    /// @notice Bring the borrow index up to the current block. Safe to call by anyone.\n    function accrueInterest() public {\n        uint256 elapsed = block.timestamp - lastAccrualTimestamp;\n        if (elapsed == 0) return;\n\n        // Always move the clock forward, even with no debt outstanding, so that idle time\n        // cannot be charged retroactively to the next borrower.\n        lastAccrualTimestamp = block.timestamp;\n\n        uint256 shares = totalDebtShares;\n        if (shares == 0) return;\n\n        uint256 rate = borrowRateBps;\n        if (rate == 0) return;\n\n        uint256 index = borrowIndex;\n        // Simple interest over the elapsed window, applied on top of the running index.\n        // Multiply before dividing; mulDiv carries the full 512-bit intermediate.\n        uint256 growth = Math.mulDiv(index, rate * elapsed, BPS * SECONDS_PER_YEAR);\n        if (growth == 0) return;\n\n        index += growth;\n        borrowIndex = index;\n\n        emit InterestAccrued(index, Math.mulDiv(shares, index, RAY));\n    }\n\n    /* ---------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n    /*                            Borrower actions                            */\n    /* ---------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n\n    /// @notice Lock collateral for `msg.sender`.\n    /// @dev Credits the amount actually received, so a fee-on-transfer collateral token\n    ///      cannot mint collateral out of thin air.\n    function depositCollateral(uint256 amount) external nonReentrant whenNotPaused {\n        if (amount == 0) revert ZeroAmount();\n\n        uint256 received = _pullToken(collateralToken, msg.sender, amount);\n\n        uint256 newTotal = totalCollateral + received;\n        if (newTotal > collateralCap) revert CollateralCapExceeded(newTotal, collateralCap);\n\n        totalCollateral = newTotal;\n        collateralOf[msg.sender] += received;\n\n        emit CollateralDeposited(msg.sender, received);\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Unlock collateral. The position must remain within `maxLtvBps` afterwards.\n    /// @dev Deliberately callable while paused: pausing must never trap a healthy borrower's\n    ///      collateral. It does need a live oracle price, though — see NOTES.md.\n    function withdrawCollateral(uint256 amount, address to) external nonReentrant {\n        if (amount == 0) revert ZeroAmount();\n        if (to == address(0)) revert ZeroAddress();\n\n        accrueInterest();\n\n        uint256 balance = collateralOf[msg.sender];\n        if (amount > balance) revert InsufficientCollateral();\n\n        uint256 remaining = balance - amount;\n        collateralOf[msg.sender] = remaining;\n        totalCollateral -= amount;\n\n        _requireWithinMaxLtv(msg.sender, remaining);\n\n        collateralToken.safeTransfer(to, amount);\n\n        emit CollateralWithdrawn(msg.sender, to, amount);\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Borrow `amount` of the debt token against already-deposited collateral.\n    function borrow(uint256 amount, address to) external nonReentrant whenNotPaused {\n        if (amount == 0) revert ZeroAmount();\n        if (to == address(0)) revert ZeroAddress();\n\n        accrueInterest();\n\n        uint256 available = debtToken.balanceOf(address(this));\n        if (amount > available) revert InsufficientLiquidity(amount, available);\n\n        // Round shares up so rounding dust is never a free loan.\n        uint256 shares = Math.mulDiv(amount, RAY, borrowIndex, Math.Rounding.Ceil);\n        if (shares == 0) revert ZeroAmount();\n\n        uint256 newTotalShares = totalDebtShares + shares;\n        uint256 newTotalDebt = Math.mulDiv(newTotalShares, borrowIndex, RAY);\n        if (newTotalDebt > borrowCap) revert BorrowCapExceeded(newTotalDebt, borrowCap);\n\n        totalDebtShares = newTotalShares;\n        debtSharesOf[msg.sender] += shares;\n\n        uint256 debt = _debtOf(msg.sender);\n        if (debt < minDebt) revert DebtBelowMinimum(debt, minDebt);\n\n        _requireWithinMaxLtv(msg.sender, collateralOf[msg.sender]);\n\n        debtToken.safeTransfer(to, amount);\n\n        emit Borrowed(msg.sender, to, amount, shares);\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Repay debt for `onBehalfOf`. Pass `type(uint256).max` to repay in full.\n    /// @dev Needs no oracle price, so a borrower can always de-risk even if the feed is down.\n    ///      Also callable while paused, for the same reason.\n    /// @return repaid The debt-token amount actually pulled from `msg.sender`.\n    function repay(uint256 amount, address onBehalfOf) external nonReentrant returns (uint256 repaid) {\n        if (amount == 0) revert ZeroAmount();\n        if (onBehalfOf == address(0)) revert ZeroAddress();\n\n        accrueInterest();\n\n        uint256 shares = debtSharesOf[onBehalfOf];\n        if (shares == 0) revert NoDebt();\n\n        uint256 debt = _sharesToDebt(shares);\n        repaid = amount > debt ? debt : amount;\n\n        uint256 sharesBurned;\n        if (repaid == debt) {\n            // Burn every share so no dust share survives a \"full\" repayment.\n            sharesBurned = shares;\n        } else {\n            // Round burned shares down: the borrower is never credited with more than they paid.\n            sharesBurned = Math.mulDiv(repaid, RAY, borrowIndex);\n            if (sharesBurned == 0) revert ZeroAmount();\n\n            uint256 remainingDebt = _sharesToDebt(shares - sharesBurned);\n            if (remainingDebt < minDebt) revert DebtBelowMinimum(remainingDebt, minDebt);\n        }\n\n        debtSharesOf[onBehalfOf] = shares - sharesBurned;\n        totalDebtShares -= sharesBurned;\n\n        uint256 received = _pullToken(debtToken, msg.sender, repaid);\n        // A debt token that delivers less than requested would let a borrower under-repay.\n        if (received != repaid) revert UnexpectedTokenBehaviour();\n\n        emit Repaid(msg.sender, onBehalfOf, repaid, sharesBurned);\n    }\n\n    /* ---------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n    /*                              Liquidation                               */\n    /* ---------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n\n    /// @notice Repay part of an unhealthy position's debt and seize the matching collateral\n    ///         plus `liquidationBonusBps`.\n    /// @param account The borrower being liquidated.\n    /// @param repayAmount Debt-token amount the caller is willing to repay. Silently clamped\n    ///        down to what the close factor and the remaining collateral allow, so an\n    ///        interest accrual or price tick between simulation and inclusion does not revert\n    ///        the transaction. Pass `type(uint256).max` to take the largest allowed bite.\n    /// @param minCollateralOut Slippage guard: revert if fewer collateral tokens are seized.\n    ///        Set this. A price move between broadcast and inclusion changes the payout.\n    /// @param to Recipient of the seized collateral.\n    /// @return debtRepaid Debt-token amount actually pulled from the caller.\n    /// @return collateralSeized Collateral-token amount actually sent to `to`.\n    function liquidate(address account, uint256 repayAmount, uint256 minCollateralOut, address to)\n        external\n        nonReentrant\n        returns (uint256 debtRepaid, uint256 collateralSeized)\n    {\n        if (account == address(0) || to == address(0)) revert ZeroAddress();\n        if (repayAmount == 0) revert ZeroAmount();\n\n        accrueInterest();\n\n        (debtRepaid, collateralSeized) = _quoteLiquidation(account, repayAmount, borrowIndex, true);\n\n        if (debtRepaid == 0 || collateralSeized == 0) revert NothingToLiquidate();\n        // `repayAmount` is a hard ceiling on what we pull, not just a target: the dust rule and\n        // the forced close-out band can both push the required repayment above what was asked.\n        if (debtRepaid > repayAmount) revert FullCloseRequired(debtRepaid);\n        if (collateralSeized < minCollateralOut) revert SlippageExceeded(collateralSeized, minCollateralOut);\n\n        uint256 sharesBurned = _applyLiquidation(account, debtRepaid, collateralSeized);\n\n        // ---- interactions ----\n        if (_pullToken(debtToken, msg.sender, debtRepaid) != debtRepaid) revert UnexpectedTokenBehaviour();\n        collateralToken.safeTransfer(to, collateralSeized);\n\n        emit Liquidated(msg.sender, account, debtRepaid, collateralSeized, sharesBurned);\n    }\n\n    /// @dev State updates for a liquidation. Split out of `liquidate` to keep the stack shallow.\n    /// @return sharesBurned Debt shares removed from `account`.\n    function _applyLiquidation(address account, uint256 debtRepaid, uint256 collateralSeized)\n        internal\n        returns (uint256 sharesBurned)\n    {\n        uint256 shares = debtSharesOf[account];\n\n        // Round burned shares up so rounding never costs the protocol; cap at the balance.\n        sharesBurned = Math.mulDiv(debtRepaid, RAY, borrowIndex, Math.Rounding.Ceil);\n        if (sharesBurned > shares) sharesBurned = shares;\n\n        uint256 remainingShares = shares - sharesBurned;\n        uint256 remainingCollateral = collateralOf[account] - collateralSeized;\n\n        debtSharesOf[account] = remainingShares;\n        totalDebtShares -= sharesBurned;\n        collateralOf[account] = remainingCollateral;\n        totalCollateral -= collateralSeized;\n\n        if (remainingCollateral == 0 && remainingShares != 0) {\n            emit BadDebtRecorded(account, _sharesToDebt(remainingShares), remainingShares);\n        }\n    }\n\n    /// @dev Shared liquidation math for `liquidate` and `previewLiquidation`.\n    /// @param revertIfHealthy When true, revert on a healthy position; when false, return zeros.\n    function _quoteLiquidation(address account, uint256 repayAmount, uint256 index, bool revertIfHealthy)\n        internal\n        view\n        returns (uint256 debtRepaid, uint256 collateralSeized)\n    {\n        uint256 shares = debtSharesOf[account];\n        if (shares == 0) {\n            if (revertIfHealthy) revert NoDebt();\n            return (0, 0);\n        }\n\n        uint256 collateral = collateralOf[account];\n        uint256 debt = _sharesToDebt(shares, index);\n        uint256 price = oracle.price();\n        uint256 collateralValue = _collateralValue(collateral, price);\n\n        // Health is checked at the liquidation threshold, not the max LTV.\n        uint256 hf = _healthFactor(collateralValue, debt);\n        if (hf >= HEALTH_FACTOR_SCALE) {\n            if (revertIfHealthy) revert PositionHealthy(hf);\n            return (0, 0);\n        }\n\n        // Close factor: a partial bite while the collateral still covers debt-plus-bonus,\n        // an all-or-nothing close-out below that.\n        //\n        // The cutoff is not arbitrary. A partial liquidation moves the health factor from\n        // Cv/D to (Cv - r(1+b))/(D - r), which is an improvement only while Cv > D * (1 + b).\n        // At or below that point every partial bite drives the position *further* underwater\n        // while leaving it liquidatable: repeated liquidations grind a solvent position down\n        // into bad debt instead of restoring it. So in that band the whole position must be\n        // closed at once — merely *permitting* a full close is not enough, since a liquidator\n        // would still be free to take a small, position-degrading bite.\n        uint256 fullCloseAt = Math.mulDiv(debt, BPS + liquidationBonusBps, BPS);\n        if (collateralValue <= fullCloseAt) {\n            debtRepaid = debt;\n        } else {\n            uint256 maxRepay = Math.mulDiv(debt, closeFactorBps, BPS);\n            debtRepaid = repayAmount > maxRepay ? maxRepay : repayAmount;\n\n            // Never leave a position with dust debt that nobody would pay gas to clear.\n            if (debt - debtRepaid < minDebt) debtRepaid = debt;\n        }\n\n        // Collateral owed = repaid value grossed up by the bonus, converted at the oracle price.\n        // Round down: the borrower keeps rounding dust, not the liquidator.\n        collateralSeized = _debtValueToCollateral(Math.mulDiv(debtRepaid, BPS + liquidationBonusBps, BPS), price);\n\n        if (collateralSeized > collateral) {\n            // Not enough collateral left to pay the full bonus. Take all of it.\n            collateralSeized = collateral;\n            uint256 seizedValue = _collateralValue(collateralSeized, price);\n\n            if (seizedValue >= debt) {\n                // The collateral is still worth more than the debt; only the *bonus* does not\n                // fit. Clear the debt in full rather than scaling the repayment down — scaling\n                // down here would turn a solvent position into a protocol loss purely to top\n                // up the liquidator's bonus. They still profit (seizedValue - debt), just at\n                // less than the headline rate.\n                debtRepaid = debt;\n            } else {\n                // Genuinely insolvent: repay as much as the collateral covers at the full\n                // bonus. Whatever debt is left over is a loss to the supply side.\n                debtRepaid = Math.mulDiv(seizedValue, BPS, BPS + liquidationBonusBps);\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    /* ---------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n    /*                                  Views                                 */\n    /* ---------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n\n    /// @notice Current debt of `account`, including accrued interest, in debt-token units.\n    function debtOf(address account) external view returns (uint256) {\n        return _sharesToDebt(debtSharesOf[account], _currentIndex());\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Total outstanding debt including accrued interest.\n    function totalDebt() external view returns (uint256) {\n        return Math.mulDiv(totalDebtShares, _currentIndex(), RAY);\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Debt tokens sitting idle and available to borrow.\n    function availableLiquidity() external view returns (uint256) {\n        return debtToken.balanceOf(address(this));\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Value of `account`'s collateral, in debt-token units, at the current price.\n    function collateralValueOf(address account) external view returns (uint256) {\n        return _collateralValue(collateralOf[account], oracle.price());\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Health factor, scaled by 1e18.\n    ///         `collateralValue * liquidationThreshold / debt`.\n    ///         `< 1e18` means liquidatable. `type(uint256).max` means no debt.\n    function healthFactor(address account) external view returns (uint256) {\n        uint256 debt = _sharesToDebt(debtSharesOf[account], _currentIndex());\n        return _healthFactor(_collateralValue(collateralOf[account], oracle.price()), debt);\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Additional debt `account` could take on right now, in debt-token units.\n    function maxBorrowable(address account) external view returns (uint256) {\n        uint256 limit = Math.mulDiv(_collateralValue(collateralOf[account], oracle.price()), maxLtvBps, BPS);\n        uint256 debt = _sharesToDebt(debtSharesOf[account], _currentIndex());\n        if (debt >= limit) return 0;\n        uint256 headroom = limit - debt;\n        uint256 available = debtToken.balanceOf(address(this));\n        return headroom > available ? available : headroom;\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Collateral `account` could withdraw right now, in collateral-token units.\n    function maxWithdrawable(address account) external view returns (uint256) {\n        uint256 collateral = collateralOf[account];\n        uint256 debt = _sharesToDebt(debtSharesOf[account], _currentIndex());\n        if (debt == 0) return collateral;\n\n        uint256 price = oracle.price();\n        // Collateral value that must stay behind to keep debt at or under max LTV.\n        uint256 requiredValue = Math.mulDiv(debt, BPS, maxLtvBps, Math.Rounding.Ceil);\n        uint256 requiredCollateral = _debtValueToCollateral(requiredValue, price, Math.Rounding.Ceil);\n        if (requiredCollateral >= collateral) return 0;\n        return collateral - requiredCollateral;\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Preview a liquidation without sending a transaction.\n    /// @return debtRepaid Debt-token amount the liquidator would pay.\n    /// @return collateralSeized Collateral-token amount they would receive.\n    function previewLiquidation(address account, uint256 repayAmount)\n        external\n        view\n        returns (uint256 debtRepaid, uint256 collateralSeized)\n    {\n        if (repayAmount == 0) return (0, 0);\n        return _quoteLiquidation(account, repayAmount, _currentIndex(), false);\n    }\n\n    /* ---------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n    /*                            Owner: liquidity                            */\n    /* ---------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n\n    /// @notice Supply debt tokens for borrowers to draw on.\n    function supply(uint256 amount) external nonReentrant onlyOwner {\n        if (amount == 0) revert ZeroAmount();\n        uint256 received = _pullToken(debtToken, msg.sender, amount);\n        emit LiquiditySupplied(msg.sender, received);\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Withdraw idle debt tokens (principal plus accrued interest that has been repaid).\n    /// @dev Bounded by the contract's own balance, so it can never touch borrower collateral\n    ///      or claw back an outstanding loan.\n    function withdrawSupply(uint256 amount, address to) external nonReentrant onlyOwner {\n        if (amount == 0) revert ZeroAmount();\n        if (to == address(0)) revert ZeroAddress();\n        uint256 available = debtToken.balanceOf(address(this));\n        if (amount > available) revert InsufficientLiquidity(amount, available);\n        debtToken.safeTransfer(to, amount);\n        emit LiquidityWithdrawn(to, amount);\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Clear the residual debt of a position that has been fully liquidated and has\n    ///         no collateral left. Accounting only: the loss has already been taken by the\n    ///         supply side. Lets the borrower's address be reused.\n    function writeOffBadDebt(address account) external onlyOwner {\n        accrueInterest();\n        uint256 collateral = collateralOf[account];\n        if (collateral != 0) revert PositionStillCollateralised(collateral);\n\n        uint256 shares = debtSharesOf[account];\n        if (shares == 0) revert NoDebt();\n\n        uint256 amount = _sharesToDebt(shares);\n        debtSharesOf[account] = 0;\n        totalDebtShares -= shares;\n\n        emit BadDebtWrittenOff(account, amount, shares);\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Recover tokens accidentally sent here. Cannot touch the collateral or debt\n    ///         token, so no owner action can reach user funds.\n    function sweep(address token, address to, uint256 amount) external onlyOwner {\n        if (token == address(collateralToken) || token == address(debtToken)) revert InvalidParameters();\n        if (to == address(0)) revert ZeroAddress();\n        IERC20(token).safeTransfer(to, amount);\n    }\n\n    /* ---------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n    /*                           Owner: parameters                            */\n    /* ---------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n\n    /// @notice Update the flat annual borrow rate. Accrues at the old rate first, so the\n    ///         change cannot be applied retroactively.\n    function setBorrowRateBps(uint256 newRateBps) external onlyOwner {\n        if (newRateBps > MAX_BORROW_RATE_BPS) revert InvalidParameters();\n        accrueInterest();\n        emit BorrowRateSet(borrowRateBps, newRateBps);\n        borrowRateBps = newRateBps;\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Update the ceiling on total outstanding debt. Lowering it below current debt\n    ///         only blocks new borrows; it never makes an existing position liquidatable.\n    function setBorrowCap(uint256 newCap) external onlyOwner {\n        emit BorrowCapSet(borrowCap, newCap);\n        borrowCap = newCap;\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Update the ceiling on total collateral held.\n    function setCollateralCap(uint256 newCap) external onlyOwner {\n        emit CollateralCapSet(collateralCap, newCap);\n        collateralCap = newCap;\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Update the minimum position size.\n    function setMinDebt(uint256 newMinDebt) external onlyOwner {\n        if (newMinDebt == 0) revert InvalidParameters();\n        emit MinDebtSet(minDebt, newMinDebt);\n        minDebt = newMinDebt;\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Stop new collateral deposits and new borrows.\n    /// @dev Repay, withdraw, and liquidate stay open while paused, by design: the pause is a\n    ///      brake on new risk, not a freeze on user funds.\n    function pause() external onlyOwner {\n        _pause();\n    }\n\n    function unpause() external onlyOwner {\n        _unpause();\n    }\n\n    /* ---------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n    /*                                Internals                               */\n    /* ---------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n\n    /// @dev Pull `amount` from `from` and return the amount actually received. Guards against\n    ///      a fee-on-transfer or otherwise lossy token being credited at face value.\n    function _pullToken(IERC20 token, address from, uint256 amount) internal returns (uint256) {\n        uint256 before = token.balanceOf(address(this));\n        token.safeTransferFrom(from, address(this), amount);\n        uint256 received = token.balanceOf(address(this)) - before;\n        if (received == 0) revert UnexpectedTokenBehaviour();\n        return received;\n    }\n\n    /// @dev Index brought forward to `block.timestamp` without writing storage. Views use this\n    ///      so they agree with what a state-changing call in the same block would see.\n    function _currentIndex() internal view returns (uint256) {\n        uint256 elapsed = block.timestamp - lastAccrualTimestamp;\n        uint256 index = borrowIndex;\n        if (elapsed == 0 || totalDebtShares == 0 || borrowRateBps == 0) return index;\n        return index + Math.mulDiv(index, borrowRateBps * elapsed, BPS * SECONDS_PER_YEAR);\n    }\n\n    /// @dev Debt owed for a share balance at the stored index. Rounds up, against the borrower.\n    function _sharesToDebt(uint256 shares) internal view returns (uint256) {\n        return _sharesToDebt(shares, borrowIndex);\n    }\n\n    function _sharesToDebt(uint256 shares, uint256 index) internal pure returns (uint256) {\n        if (shares == 0) return 0;\n        return Math.mulDiv(shares, index, RAY, Math.Rounding.Ceil);\n    }\n\n    function _debtOf(address account) internal view returns (uint256) {\n        return _sharesToDebt(debtSharesOf[account], borrowIndex);\n    }\n\n    /// @dev Raw collateral amount -> value in debt-token units, at `price`. Rounds down.\n    function _collateralValue(uint256 amount, uint256 price) internal view returns (uint256) {\n        if (amount == 0) return 0;\n        return Math.mulDiv(amount, price, priceScale);\n    }\n\n    /// @dev Value in debt-token units -> raw collateral amount, at `price`. Rounds down.\n    function _debtValueToCollateral(uint256 value, uint256 price) internal view returns (uint256) {\n        return _debtValueToCollateral(value, price, Math.Rounding.Floor);\n    }\n\n    function _debtValueToCollateral(uint256 value, uint256 price, Math.Rounding rounding)\n        internal\n        view\n        returns (uint256)\n    {\n        if (value == 0) return 0;\n        return Math.mulDiv(value, priceScale, price, rounding);\n    }\n\n    /// @dev `collateralValue * liquidationThreshold / debt`, scaled by 1e18.\n    function _healthFactor(uint256 collateralValue, uint256 debt) internal view returns (uint256) {\n        if (debt == 0) return MAX_HEALTH_FACTOR;\n        return Math.mulDiv(\n            Math.mulDiv(collateralValue, liquidationThresholdBps, BPS), HEALTH_FACTOR_SCALE, debt\n        );\n    }\n\n    /// @dev Revert unless `account`'s debt is within `maxLtvBps` of `collateral`'s value.\n    ///      Reverts on a stale oracle, which is why `repay` never calls this.\n    function _requireWithinMaxLtv(address account, uint256 collateral) internal view {\n        uint256 debt = _debtOf(account);\n        if (debt == 0) return;\n\n        uint256 collateralValue = _collateralValue(collateral, oracle.price());\n        uint256 limit = Math.mulDiv(collateralValue, maxLtvBps, BPS);\n        if (debt > limit) revert PositionUnhealthy(_healthFactor(collateralValue, debt));\n    }\n}\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":383,"oldLines":10,"newStart":383,"newLines":13,"lines":["     /// @notice Repay part of an unhealthy position's debt and seize the matching collateral","     ///         plus `liquidationBonusBps`.","     /// @param account The borrower being liquidated.","-    /// @param repayAmount Debt-token amount the caller is willing to repay. feed.setAnswer(1_600e8);\n\n        vm.startPrank(bob);\n        usdc.approve(address(market), type(uint256).max);\n        (uint256 repaid,) = market.liquidate(alice, type(uint256).max, 0, bob);\n        vm.stopPrank();\n\n        assertEq(repaid, 7_000e6);\n        uint256 remaining = market.debtOf(alice);\n        assertTrue(remaining == 0 || remaining >= market.minDebt());\n    }\n\n    function test_repayAmountIsAHardCeiling() public {\n        _deposit(alice, 10e18);\n        _borrow(alice, 14_000e6);\n        // Cv = $14,500 < debt * 1.05, so the whole position must be closed at once.\n        feed.setAnswer(1_450e8);\n\n        (uint256 required,) = market.previewLiquidation(alice, 1_000e6);\n        assertEq(required, 14_000e6);\n\n        vm.startPrank(bob);\n        usdc.approve(address(market), type(uint256).max);\n        // Asking to repay only $1,000 must revert rather than pull the full $14,000.\n        vm.expectRevert(abi.encodeWithSelector(LendingMarket.FullCloseRequired.selector, 14_000e6));\n        market.liquidate(alice, 1_000e6, 0, bob);\n        vm.stopPrank();\n\n        assertEq(usdc.balanceOf(bob), 1_000_000e6); // nothing pulled\n    }","originalFile":"// SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT\npragma solidity 0.8.28;\n\nimport {Test} from \"forge-std/Test.sol\";\n\nimport {ChainlinkPriceOracle} from \"../src/ChainlinkPriceOracle.sol\";\nimport {LendingMarket} from \"../src/LendingMarket.sol\";\nimport {MockAggregator} from \"./mocks/MockAggregator.sol\";\nimport {MockERC20} from \"./mocks/MockERC20.sol\";\n\ncontract LendingMarketTest is Test {\n    MockERC20 weth;\n    MockERC20 usdc;\n    MockAggregator feed;\n    ChainlinkPriceOracle oracle;\n    LendingMarket market;\n\n    address owner = address(0xA11CE);\n    address alice = address(0xBEEF);\n    address bob = address(0xCAFE);\n\n    uint256 constant START_PRICE = 2_000e8; // $2000 / ETH, 8 decimals\n\n    function setUp() public {\n        vm.warp(1_700_000_000);\n\n        weth = new MockERC20(\"Wrapped Ether\", \"WETH\", 18);\n        usdc = new MockERC20(\"USD Coin\", \"USDC\", 6);\n        feed = new MockAggregator(8, int256(START_PRICE));\n        oracle = new ChainlinkPriceOracle(address(feed), 3900, 100e8, 100_000e8);\n\n        market = new LendingMarket(\n            LendingMarket.Params({\n                owner: owner,\n                collateralToken: address(weth),\n                debtToken: address(usdc),\n                oracle: address(oracle),\n                maxLtvBps: 7_000,\n                liquidationThresholdBps: 8_500,\n                liquidationBonusBps: 500,\n                closeFactorBps: 5_000,\n                borrowRateBps: 500,\n                borrowCap: 2_000_000e6,\n                collateralCap: 1_000e18,\n                minDebt: 500e6\n            })\n        );\n\n        usdc.mint(owner, 1_000_000e6);\n        vm.startPrank(owner);\n        usdc.approve(address(market), type(uint256).max);\n        market.supply(1_000_000e6);\n        vm.stopPrank();\n\n        weth.mint(alice, 100e18);\n        usdc.mint(bob, 1_000_000e6);\n    }\n\n    /* ------------------------------ helpers ------------------------------ */\n\n    function _deposit(address who, uint256 amount) internal {\n        vm.startPrank(who);\n        weth.approve(address(market), amount);\n        market.depositCollateral(amount);\n        vm.stopPrank();\n    }\n\n    function _borrow(address who, uint256 amount) internal {\n        vm.prank(who);\n        market.borrow(amount, who);\n    }\n\n    /* ------------------------------- scaling ----------------------------- */\n\n    function test_priceScale_derivedFromDecimals() public view {\n        // 18 (WETH) + 8 (feed) - 6 (USDC)\n        assertEq(market.priceScale(), 1e20);\n    }\n\n    function test_collateralValue_usesSixDecimals() public {\n        _deposit(alice, 1e18);\n        // 1 WETH @ $2000 => 2000 USDC (6 decimals)\n        assertEq(market.collateralValueOf(alice), 2_000e6);\n    }\n\n    /* ------------------------------ borrowing ---------------------------- */\n\n    function test_borrowUpTo70Percent() public {\n        _deposit(alice, 10e18); // $20,000\n        assertEq(market.maxBorrowable(alice), 14_000e6);\n\n        _borrow(alice, 14_000e6);\n        assertEq(usdc.balanceOf(alice), 14_000e6);\n        assertEq(market.debtOf(alice), 14_000e6);\n    }\n\n    function test_borrowAbove70PercentReverts() public {\n        _deposit(alice, 10e18);\n        vm.prank(alice);\n        vm.expectRevert();\n        market.borrow(14_000e6 + 1, alice);\n    }\n\n    function test_borrowBelowMinDebtReverts() public {\n        _deposit(alice, 10e18);\n        vm.prank(alice);\n        vm.expectRevert(abi.encodeWithSelector(LendingMarket.DebtBelowMinimum.selector, 499e6, 500e6));\n        market.borrow(499e6, alice);\n    }\n\n    function test_borrowRespectsCap() public {\n        vm.prank(owner);\n        market.setBorrowCap(1_000e6);\n\n        _deposit(alice, 10e18);\n        vm.prank(alice);\n        vm.expectRevert(abi.encodeWithSelector(LendingMarket.BorrowCapExceeded.selector, 1_001e6, 1_000e6));\n        market.borrow(1_001e6, alice);\n    }\n\n    /* ------------------------------- interest ---------------------------- */\n\n    function test_interestAccruesAtFlatRate() public {\n        _deposit(alice, 10e18);\n        _borrow(alice, 10_000e6);\n\n        vm.warp(block.timestamp + 365 days);\n        // 5% flat on 10,000 USDC == 500 USDC (rounding up by at most 1 unit)\n        assertApproxEqAbs(market.debtOf(alice), 10_500e6, 1);\n    }\n\n    function test_noRetroactiveInterestBeforeFirstBorrow() public {\n        vm.warp(block.timestamp + 365 days);\n        feed.setAnswer(int256(START_PRICE)); // refresh the feed after the warp\n        _deposit(alice, 10e18);\n        _borrow(alice, 10_000e6);\n        assertApproxEqAbs(market.debtOf(alice), 10_000e6, 1);\n    }\n\n    /* -------------------------- repay / withdraw ------------------------- */\n\n    function test_repayFullBurnsAllShares() public {\n        _deposit(alice, 10e18);\n        _borrow(alice, 10_000e6);\n\n        vm.warp(block.timestamp + 30 days);\n        uint256 debt = market.debtOf(alice);\n\n        usdc.mint(alice, debt);\n        vm.startPrank(alice);\n        usdc.approve(address(market), type(uint256).max);\n        market.repay(type(uint256).max, alice);\n        vm.stopPrank();\n\n        assertEq(market.debtOf(alice), 0);\n        assertEq(market.debtSharesOf(alice), 0);\n        assertEq(market.totalDebtShares(), 0);\n        assertGe(usdc.balanceOf(address(market)), 1_000_000e6 - 10_000e6 + debt);\n    }\n\n    function test_repayWorksWhileOracleIsDown() public {\n        _deposit(alice, 10e18);\n        _borrow(alice, 10_000e6);\n\n        // Feed goes stale — borrow/withdraw must fail, repay must still work.\n        vm.warp(block.timestamp + 10 days);\n\n        vm.prank(alice);\n        vm.expectRevert();\n        market.withdrawCollateral(1e18, alice);\n\n        usdc.mint(alice, 1_000e6);\n        vm.startPrank(alice);\n        usdc.approve(address(market), type(uint256).max);\n        market.repay(type(uint256).max, alice);\n        vm.stopPrank();\n        assertEq(market.debtOf(alice), 0);\n    }\n\n    function test_withdrawBlockedIfItBreachesLtv() public {\n        _deposit(alice, 10e18);\n        _borrow(alice, 14_000e6);\n\n        vm.prank(alice);\n        vm.expectRevert();\n        market.withdrawCollateral(1, alice);\n    }\n\n    function test_withdrawAllWhenNoDebt() public {\n        _deposit(alice, 10e18);\n        vm.prank(alice);\n        market.withdrawCollateral(10e18, alice);\n        assertEq(weth.balanceOf(alice), 100e18);\n        assertEq(market.totalCollateral(), 0);\n    }\n\n    function test_pauseDoesNotTrapUsers() public {\n        _deposit(alice, 10e18);\n        _borrow(alice, 10_000e6);\n\n        vm.prank(owner);\n        market.pause();\n\n        // New risk blocked...\n        vm.startPrank(alice);\n        weth.approve(address(market), 1e18);\n        vm.expectRevert();\n        market.depositCollateral(1e18);\n        vm.expectRevert();\n        market.borrow(1e6, alice);\n\n        // ...but exits stay open.\n        usdc.approve(address(market), type(uint256).max);\n        market.repay(type(uint256).max, alice);\n        market.withdrawCollateral(10e18, alice);\n        vm.stopPrank();\n    }\n\n    /* ------------------------------ liquidation -------------------------- */\n\n    function test_healthyPositionCannotBeLiquidated() public {\n        _deposit(alice, 10e18);\n        _borrow(alice, 14_000e6);\n\n        vm.startPrank(bob);\n        usdc.approve(address(market), type(uint256).max);\n        vm.expectRevert();\n        market.liquidate(alice, 1_000e6, 0, bob);\n        vm.stopPrank();\n    }\n\n    function test_liquidationPaysFivePercentBonus() public {\n        _deposit(alice, 10e18);\n        _borrow(alice, 14_000e6); // 70% of $20,000\n\n        // ETH to $1600 => collateral $16,000, threshold value $13,600 < debt $14,000\n        feed.setAnswer(1_600e8);\n        assertLt(market.healthFactor(alice), 1e18);\n\n        (uint256 previewRepaid, uint256 previewSeized) = market.previewLiquidation(alice, 7_000e6);\n\n        vm.startPrank(bob);\n        usdc.approve(address(market), type(uint256).max);\n        (uint256 repaid, uint256 seized) = market.liquidate(alice, 7_000e6, previewSeized, bob);\n        vm.stopPrank();\n\n        assertEq(repaid, previewRepaid);\n        assertEq(seized, previewSeized);\n        assertEq(repaid, 7_000e6); // close factor = 50% of 14,000\n        // 7000 USDC * 1.05 / 1600 = 4.59375 WETH\n        assertEq(seized, 4.59375e18);\n        assertEq(weth.balanceOf(bob), seized);\n        assertGe(market.healthFactor(alice), 1e18); // restored to healthy\n    }\n\n    function test_closeFactorCapsRepayment() public {\n        _deposit(alice, 10e18);\n        _borrow(alice, 14_000e6);\n        feed.setAnswer(1_600e8);\n\n        vm.startPrank(bob);\n        usdc.approve(address(market), type(uint256).max);\n        (uint256 repaid,) = market.liquidate(alice, type(uint256).max, 0, bob);\n        vm.stopPrank();\n\n        assertEq(repaid, 7_000e6);\n    }\n\n    function test_underwaterPositionAllowsFullLiquidation() public {\n        _deposit(alice, 10e18);\n        _borrow(alice, 14_000e6);\n\n        // ETH to $1300 => collateral $13,000 < debt $14,000\n        feed.setAnswer(1_300e8);\n\n        vm.startPrank(bob);\n        usdc.approve(address(market), type(uint256).max);\n        (uint256 repaid, uint256 seized) = market.liquidate(alice, type(uint256).max, 0, bob);\n        vm.stopPrank();\n\n        // Collateral runs out before the full debt can be repaid at a 5% bonus.\n        assertEq(seized, 10e18);\n        assertEq(repaid, uint256(13_000e6) * 10_000 / 10_500);\n        assertEq(market.collateralOf(alice), 0);\n        assertGt(market.debtOf(alice), 0); // residual bad debt\n    }\n\n    function test_slippageGuardRevertsOnPriceMove() public {\n        _deposit(alice, 10e18);\n        _borrow(alice, 14_000e6);\n        feed.setAnswer(1_600e8);\n\n        (, uint256 expected) = market.previewLiquidation(alice, 7_000e6);\n\n        // Price recovers before inclusion; the same repayment now buys less WETH.\n        feed.setAnswer(1_610e8);\n\n        vm.startPrank(bob);\n        usdc.approve(address(market), type(uint256).max);\n        vm.expectRevert();\n        market.liquidate(alice, 7_000e6, expected, bob);\n        vm.stopPrank();\n    }\n\n    function test_liquidationNeverLeavesDustDebt() public {\n        _deposit(alice, 10e18);\n        _borrow(alice, 14_000e6);\n        feed.setAnswer(1_600e8);\n\n        vm.startPrank(bob);\n        usdc.approve(address(market), type(uint256).max);\n        // Ask to repay just under the full debt: the dust rule should round it up to all of it.\n        (uint256 repaid,) = market.liquidate(alice, 14_000e6 - 1e6, 0, bob);\n        vm.stopPrank();\n\n        assertEq(repaid, market.BPS() > 0 ? repaid : 0);\n        uint256 remaining = market.debtOf(alice);\n        assertTrue(remaining == 0 || remaining >= market.minDebt());\n    }\n\n    function test_writeOffBadDebt() public {\n        _deposit(alice, 10e18);\n        _borrow(alice, 14_000e6);\n        feed.setAnswer(1_300e8);\n\n        vm.startPrank(bob);\n        usdc.approve(address(market), type(uint256).max);\n        market.liquidate(alice, type(uint256).max, 0, bob);\n        vm.stopPrank();\n\n        assertGt(market.debtOf(alice), 0);\n        vm.prank(owner);\n        market.writeOffBadDebt(alice);\n        assertEq(market.debtOf(alice), 0);\n        assertEq(market.totalDebtShares(), 0);\n    }\n\n    /* -------------------------------- oracle ----------------------------- */\n\n    function test_stalePriceReverts() public {\n        vm.warp(block.timestamp + 4000);\n        vm.expectRevert();\n        oracle.price();\n    }\n\n    function test_nonPositiveAnswerReverts() public {\n        feed.setAnswer(0);\n        vm.expectRevert(ChainlinkPriceOracle.NonPositiveAnswer.selector);\n        oracle.price();\n    }\n\n    function test_outOfBandAnswerReverts() public {\n        feed.setAnswer(50e8); // below the $100 floor\n        vm.expectRevert(abi.encodeWithSelector(ChainlinkPriceOracle.PriceOutOfBounds.selector, 50e8));\n        oracle.price();\n    }\n\n    function test_incompleteRoundReverts() public {\n        feed.setAnsweredInRound(0);\n        vm.expectRevert(ChainlinkPriceOracle.StaleRound.selector);\n        oracle.price();\n    }\n\n    /* ----------------------------- access control ------------------------ */\n\n    function test_onlyOwnerCanTouchParameters() public {\n        vm.startPrank(alice);\n        vm.expectRevert();\n        market.setBorrowRateBps(100);\n        vm.expectRevert();\n        market.pause();\n        vm.expectRevert();\n        market.withdrawSupply(1e6, alice);\n        vm.expectRevert();\n        market.writeOffBadDebt(bob);\n        vm.stopPrank();\n    }\n\n    function test_rateIsCapped() public {\n        vm.prank(owner);\n        vm.expectRevert(LendingMarket.InvalidParameters.selector);\n        market.setBorrowRateBps(5_001);\n    }\n\n    function test_ownerCannotSweepUserAssets() public {\n        vm.startPrank(owner);\n        vm.expectRevert(LendingMarket.InvalidParameters.selector);\n        market.sweep(address(weth), owner, 1);\n        vm.expectRevert(LendingMarket.InvalidParameters.selector);\n        market.sweep(address(usdc), owner, 1);\n        vm.stopPrank();\n    }\n\n    function test_ownerCannotWithdrawCollateralViaSupplyWithdrawal() public {\n        _deposit(alice, 10e18);\n        // withdrawSupply is bounded by the USDC balance and never touches WETH.\n        vm.prank(owner);\n        market.withdrawSupply(1_000_000e6, owner);\n        assertEq(weth.balanceOf(address(market)), 10e18);\n        assertEq(market.collateralOf(alice), 10e18);\n    }\n\n    /* -------------------------------- fuzz ------------------------------- */\n\n    /// @dev Solvency invariant: a borrower can never walk away with more debt-token value\n    ///      than their collateral was worth at the max LTV.\n    function testFuzz_cannotBorrowBeyondLtv(uint256 collateral, uint256 borrowAmount) public {\n        collateral = bound(collateral, 1e15, 100e18);\n        uint256 limit = collateral * START_PRICE / 1e20 * 7_000 / 10_000;\n        vm.assume(limit >= 500e6);\n        borrowAmount = bound(borrowAmount, 500e6, limit + 1e6);\n\n        _deposit(alice, collateral);\n        vm.prank(alice);\n        if (borrowAmount > limit) {\n            vm.expectRevert();\n            market.borrow(borrowAmount, alice);\n        } else {\n            market.borrow(borrowAmount, alice);\n            assertGe(market.healthFactor(alice), 1e18);\n        }\n    }\n\n    /// @dev While a position is still solvent (collateral worth at least the debt), a\n    ///      liquidation must never make it worse: either health improves, or the position is\n    ///      closed out entirely. This is the property the full-close band exists to preserve.\n    function testFuzz_solventLiquidationNeverDegradesPosition(uint256 newPrice, uint256 repayAmount) public {\n        _deposit(alice, 10e18);\n        _borrow(alice, 14_000e6);\n\n        // Cv >= $14,000 == debt, so the position is unhealthy but not yet insolvent.\n        newPrice = bound(newPrice, 1_400e8, 1_640e8);\n        feed.setAnswer(int256(newPrice));\n        vm.assume(market.healthFactor(alice) < 1e18);\n\n        repayAmount = bound(repayAmount, 1e6, 14_000e6);\n        (uint256 previewRepaid,) = market.previewLiquidation(alice, repayAmount);\n        vm.assume(previewRepaid > 0);\n\n        uint256 hfBefore = market.healthFactor(alice);\n        vm.startPrank(bob);\n        usdc.approve(address(market), type(uint256).max);\n        market.liquidate(alice, repayAmount, 0, bob);\n        vm.stopPrank();\n\n        assertTrue(market.healthFactor(alice) >= hfBefore || market.collateralOf(alice) == 0);\n        // A solvent position must never be turned into bad debt.\n        assertTrue(market.debtOf(alice) == 0 || market.collateralOf(alice) > 0);\n    }\n\n    /// @dev Once a position is genuinely insolvent, paying any bonus at all necessarily grows\n    ///      the shortfall — no liquidation rule avoids that. What must still hold is that\n    ///      every liquidation makes strict progress and that `previewLiquidation` is exact.\n    function testFuzz_insolventLiquidationMakesProgress(uint256 newPrice, uint256 repayAmount) public {\n        _deposit(alice, 10e18);\n        _borrow(alice, 14_000e6);\n\n        // Cv < $14,000 == debt: underwater.\n        newPrice = bound(newPrice, 900e8, 1_399e8);\n        feed.setAnswer(int256(newPrice));\n\n        repayAmount = bound(repayAmount, 1e6, 14_000e6);\n        (uint256 previewRepaid, uint256 previewSeized) = market.previewLiquidation(alice, repayAmount);\n        vm.assume(previewRepaid > 0);\n\n        uint256 debtBefore = market.debtOf(alice);\n        uint256 collateralBefore = market.collateralOf(alice);\n\n        vm.startPrank(bob);\n        usdc.approve(address(market), type(uint256).max);\n        (uint256 repaid, uint256 seized) = market.liquidate(alice, repayAmount, 0, bob);\n        vm.stopPrank();\n\n        assertEq(repaid, previewRepaid);\n        assertEq(seized, previewSeized);\n        assertLt(market.debtOf(alice), debtBefore);\n        assertLt(market.collateralOf(alice), collateralBefore);\n        assertEq(weth.balanceOf(bob), seized);\n    }\n\n    /// @dev Repeated liquidations must converge: a position can never be ground down\n    ///      forever while staying liquidatable.\n    function test_liquidationConvergesInTheThinBand() public {\n        _deposit(alice, 10e18);\n        _borrow(alice, 14_000e6);\n\n        // Cv = $14,500, debt = $14,000. Cv < debt * 1.05, so a capped 50% liquidation\n        // would make health *worse*; the full-close rule must kick in instead.\n        feed.setAnswer(1_450e8);\n        assertLt(market.healthFactor(alice), 1e18);\n\n        vm.startPrank(bob);\n        usdc.approve(address(market), type(uint256).max);\n        (uint256 repaid,) = market.liquidate(alice, type(uint256).max, 0, bob);\n        vm.stopPrank();\n\n        assertEq(repaid, 14_000e6); // whole debt, not the 50% close factor\n        assertEq(market.debtOf(alice), 0); // no bad debt manufactured\n        // All 10 WETH ($14,500) goes to the liquidator for a $14,000 repayment: a 3.6%\n        // bonus rather than the headline 5%, because that is all the collateral supports.\n        assertEq(weth.balanceOf(bob), 10e18);\n        assertEq(market.collateralOf(alice), 0);\n    }\n}\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":309,"oldLines":15,"newStart":309,"newLines":33,"lines":[" ","         vm.startPrank(bob);","         usdc.approve(address(market), type(uint256).max);","-        // Ask to repay just under the full debt: the dust rule should round it up to all of it.","-        (uint256 repaid,) = market.liquidate(alice, 14_000e6 - 1e6, 0, bob);","+        (uint256 repaid,) = market.liquidate(alice, type(uint256).max, 0, bob);","         vm.stopPrank();"," ","-        assertEq(repaid, market.BPS() > 0 ? repaid : 0);","+        assertEq(repaid, 7_000e6);","         uint256 remaining = market.debtOf(alice);","         assertTrue(remaining == 0 || remaining >= market.minDebt());","     }"," ","+    function test_repayAmountIsAHardCeiling() public {","+        _deposit(alice, 10e18);","+        _borrow(alice, 14_000e6);","+        // Cv = $14,500 < debt * 1.05, so the whole position must be closed at once.","+        feed.setAnswer(1_450e8);","+","+        (uint256 required,) = market.previewLiquidation(alice, 1_000e6);","+        assertEq(required, 14_000e6);","+","+        vm.startPrank(bob);","+        usdc.approve(address(market), type(uint256).max);","+        // Asking to repay only $1,000 must revert rather than pull the full $14,000.","+        vm.expectRevert(abi.encodeWithSelector(LendingMarket.FullCloseRequired.selector, 14_000e6));","+        market.liquidate(alice, 1_000e6, 0, bob);","+        vm.stopPrank();","+","+        assertEq(usdc.balanceOf(bob), 1_000_000e6); // nothing pulled","+    }","+","     function test_writeOffBadDebt() public {","         _deposit(alice, 10e18);","         _borrow(alice, 14_000e6);"]}],"userModified":false,"replaceAll":false}}

       repayAmount = bound(repayAmount, 1e6, 14_000e6);\n        (uint256 previewRepaid,) = market.previewLiquidation(alice, repayAmount);\n        vm.assume(previewRepaid > 0);\n\n        uint256 must never be turned into bad debt.\n        assertTrue(market.debtOf(alice) == 0 || market.collateralOf(alice) > 0);\n    }","originalFile":"// SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT\npragma solidity 0.8.28;\n\nimport {Test} from \"forge-std/Test.sol\";\n\nimport {ChainlinkPriceOracle} from \"../src/ChainlinkPriceOracle.sol\";\nimport {LendingMarket} from \"../src/LendingMarket.sol\";\nimport {MockAggregator} from \"./mocks/MockAggregator.sol\";\nimport {MockERC20} from \"./mocks/MockERC20.sol\";\n\ncontract LendingMarketTest is Test {\n    MockERC20 weth;\n    MockERC20 usdc;\n    MockAggregator feed;\n    ChainlinkPriceOracle oracle;\n    LendingMarket market;\n\n    address owner = address(0xA11CE);\n    address alice = address(0xBEEF);\n    address bob = address(0xCAFE);\n\n    uint256 constant START_PRICE = 2_000e8; // $2000 / ETH, 8 decimals\n\n    function setUp() public {\n        vm.warp(1_700_000_000);\n\n        weth = new MockERC20(\"Wrapped Ether\", \"WETH\", 18);\n        usdc = new MockERC20(\"USD Coin\", \"USDC\", 6);\n        feed = new MockAggregator(8, int256(START_PRICE));\n        oracle = new ChainlinkPriceOracle(address(feed), 3900, 100e8, 100_000e8);\n\n        market = new LendingMarket(\n            LendingMarket.Params({\n                owner: owner,\n                collateralToken: address(weth),\n                debtToken: address(usdc),\n                oracle: address(oracle),\n                maxLtvBps: 7_000,\n                liquidationThresholdBps: 8_500,\n                liquidationBonusBps: 500,\n                closeFactorBps: 5_000,\n                borrowRateBps: 500,\n                borrowCap: 2_000_000e6,\n                collateralCap: 1_000e18,\n                minDebt: 500e6\n            })\n        );\n\n        usdc.mint(owner, 1_000_000e6);\n        vm.startPrank(owner);\n        usdc.approve(address(market), type(uint256).max);\n        market.supply(1_000_000e6);\n        vm.stopPrank();\n\n        weth.mint(alice, 100e18);\n        usdc.mint(bob, 1_000_000e6);\n    }\n\n    /* ------------------------------ helpers ------------------------------ */\n\n    function _deposit(address who, uint256 amount) internal {\n        vm.startPrank(who);\n        weth.approve(address(market), amount);\n        market.depositCollateral(amount);\n        vm.stopPrank();\n    }\n\n    function _borrow(address who, uint256 amount) internal {\n        vm.prank(who);\n        market.borrow(amount, who);\n    }\n\n    /* ------------------------------- scaling ----------------------------- */\n\n    function test_priceScale_derivedFromDecimals() public view {\n        // 18 (WETH) + 8 (feed) - 6 (USDC)\n        assertEq(market.priceScale(), 1e20);\n    }\n\n    function test_collateralValue_usesSixDecimals() public {\n        _deposit(alice, 1e18);\n        // 1 WETH @ $2000 => 2000 USDC (6 decimals)\n        assertEq(market.collateralValueOf(alice), 2_000e6);\n    }\n\n    /* ------------------------------ borrowing ---------------------------- */\n\n    function test_borrowUpTo70Percent() public {\n        _deposit(alice, 10e18); // $20,000\n        assertEq(market.maxBorrowable(alice), 14_000e6);\n\n        _borrow(alice, 14_000e6);\n        assertEq(usdc.balanceOf(alice), 14_000e6);\n        assertEq(market.debtOf(alice), 14_000e6);\n    }\n\n    function test_borrowAbove70PercentReverts() public {\n        _deposit(alice, 10e18);\n        vm.prank(alice);\n        vm.expectRevert();\n        market.borrow(14_000e6 + 1, alice);\n    }\n\n    function test_borrowBelowMinDebtReverts() public {\n        _deposit(alice, 10e18);\n        vm.prank(alice);\n        vm.expectRevert(abi.encodeWithSelector(LendingMarket.DebtBelowMinimum.selector, 499e6, 500e6));\n        market.borrow(499e6, alice);\n    }\n\n    function test_borrowRespectsCap() public {\n        vm.prank(owner);\n        market.setBorrowCap(1_000e6);\n\n        _deposit(alice, 10e18);\n        vm.prank(alice);\n        vm.expectRevert(abi.encodeWithSelector(LendingMarket.BorrowCapExceeded.selector, 1_001e6, 1_000e6));\n        market.borrow(1_001e6, alice);\n    }\n\n    /* ------------------------------- interest ---------------------------- */\n\n    function test_interestAccruesAtFlatRate() public {\n        _deposit(alice, 10e18);\n        _borrow(alice, 10_000e6);\n\n        vm.warp(block.timestamp + 365 days);\n        // 5% flat on 10,000 USDC == 500 USDC (rounding up by at most 1 unit)\n        assertApproxEqAbs(market.debtOf(alice), 10_500e6, 1);\n    }\n\n    function test_noRetroactiveInterestBeforeFirstBorrow() public {\n        vm.warp(block.timestamp + 365 days);\n        feed.setAnswer(int256(START_PRICE)); // refresh the feed after the warp\n        _deposit(alice, 10e18);\n        _borrow(alice, 10_000e6);\n        assertApproxEqAbs(market.debtOf(alice), 10_000e6, 1);\n    }\n\n    /* -------------------------- repay / withdraw ------------------------- */\n\n    function test_repayFullBurnsAllShares() public {\n        _deposit(alice, 10e18);\n        _borrow(alice, 10_000e6);\n\n        vm.warp(block.timestamp + 30 days);\n        uint256 debt = market.debtOf(alice);\n\n        usdc.mint(alice, debt);\n        vm.startPrank(alice);\n        usdc.approve(address(market), type(uint256).max);\n        market.repay(type(uint256).max, alice);\n        vm.stopPrank();\n\n        assertEq(market.debtOf(alice), 0);\n        assertEq(market.debtSharesOf(alice), 0);\n        assertEq(market.totalDebtShares(), 0);\n        assertGe(usdc.balanceOf(address(market)), 1_000_000e6 - 10_000e6 + debt);\n    }\n\n    function test_repayWorksWhileOracleIsDown() public {\n        _deposit(alice, 10e18);\n        _borrow(alice, 10_000e6);\n\n        // Feed goes stale — borrow/withdraw must fail, repay must still work.\n        vm.warp(block.timestamp + 10 days);\n\n        vm.prank(alice);\n        vm.expectRevert();\n        market.withdrawCollateral(1e18, alice);\n\n        usdc.mint(alice, 1_000e6);\n        vm.startPrank(alice);\n        usdc.approve(address(market), type(uint256).max);\n        market.repay(type(uint256).max, alice);\n        vm.stopPrank();\n        assertEq(market.debtOf(alice), 0);\n    }\n\n    function test_withdrawBlockedIfItBreachesLtv() public {\n        _deposit(alice, 10e18);\n        _borrow(alice, 14_000e6);\n\n        vm.prank(alice);\n        vm.expectRevert();\n        market.withdrawCollateral(1, alice);\n    }\n\n    function test_withdrawAllWhenNoDebt() public {\n        _deposit(alice, 10e18);\n        vm.prank(alice);\n        market.withdrawCollateral(10e18, alice);\n        assertEq(weth.balanceOf(alice), 100e18);\n        assertEq(market.totalCollateral(), 0);\n    }\n\n    function test_pauseDoesNotTrapUsers() public {\n        _deposit(alice, 10e18);\n        _borrow(alice, 10_000e6);\n\n        vm.prank(owner);\n        market.pause();\n\n        // New risk blocked...\n        vm.startPrank(alice);\n        weth.approve(address(market), 1e18);\n        vm.expectRevert();\n        market.depositCollateral(1e18);\n        vm.expectRevert();\n        market.borrow(1e6, alice);\n\n        // ...but exits stay open.\n        usdc.approve(address(market), type(uint256).max);\n        market.repay(type(uint256).max, alice);\n        market.withdrawCollateral(10e18, alice);\n        vm.stopPrank();\n    }\n\n    /* ------------------------------ liquidation -------------------------- */\n\n    function test_healthyPositionCannotBeLiquidated() public {\n        _deposit(alice, 10e18);\n        _borrow(alice, 14_000e6);\n\n        vm.startPrank(bob);\n        usdc.approve(address(market), type(uint256).max);\n        vm.expectRevert();\n        market.liquidate(alice, 1_000e6, 0, bob);\n        vm.stopPrank();\n    }\n\n    function test_liquidationPaysFivePercentBonus() public {\n        _deposit(alice, 10e18);\n        _borrow(alice, 14_000e6); // 70% of $20,000\n\n        // ETH to $1600 => collateral $16,000, threshold value $13,600 < debt $14,000\n        feed.setAnswer(1_600e8);\n        assertLt(market.healthFactor(alice), 1e18);\n\n        (uint256 previewRepaid, uint256 previewSeized) = market.previewLiquidation(alice, 7_000e6);\n\n        vm.startPrank(bob);\n        usdc.approve(address(market), type(uint256).max);\n        (uint256 repaid, uint256 seized) = market.liquidate(alice, 7_000e6, previewSeized, bob);\n        vm.stopPrank();\n\n        assertEq(repaid, previewRepaid);\n        assertEq(seized, previewSeized);\n        assertEq(repaid, 7_000e6); // close factor = 50% of 14,000\n        // 7000 USDC * 1.05 / 1600 = 4.59375 WETH\n        assertEq(seized, 4.59375e18);\n        assertEq(weth.balanceOf(bob), seized);\n        assertGe(market.healthFactor(alice), 1e18); // restored to healthy\n    }\n\n    function test_closeFactorCapsRepayment() public {\n        _deposit(alice, 10e18);\n        _borrow(alice, 14_000e6);\n        feed.setAnswer(1_600e8);\n\n        vm.startPrank(bob);\n        usdc.approve(address(market), type(uint256).max);\n        (uint256 repaid,) = market.liquidate(alice, type(uint256).max, 0, bob);\n        vm.stopPrank();\n\n        assertEq(repaid, 7_000e6);\n    }\n\n    function test_underwaterPositionAllowsFullLiquidation() public {\n        _deposit(alice, 10e18);\n        _borrow(alice, 14_000e6);\n\n        // ETH to $1300 => collateral $13,000 < debt $14,000\n        feed.setAnswer(1_300e8);\n\n        vm.startPrank(bob);\n        usdc.approve(address(market), type(uint256).max);\n        (uint256 repaid, uint256 seized) = market.liquidate(alice, type(uint256).max, 0, bob);\n        vm.stopPrank();\n\n        // Collateral runs out before the full debt can be repaid at a 5% bonus.\n        assertEq(seized, 10e18);\n        assertEq(repaid, uint256(13_000e6) * 10_000 / 10_500);\n        assertEq(market.collateralOf(alice), 0);\n        assertGt(market.debtOf(alice), 0); // residual bad debt\n    }\n\n    function test_slippageGuardRevertsOnPriceMove() public {\n        _deposit(alice, 10e18);\n        _borrow(alice, 14_000e6);\n        feed.setAnswer(1_600e8);\n\n        (, uint256 expected) = market.previewLiquidation(alice, 7_000e6);\n\n        // Price recovers before inclusion; the same repayment now buys less WETH.\n        feed.setAnswer(1_610e8);\n\n        vm.startPrank(bob);\n        usdc.approve(address(market), type(uint256).max);\n        vm.expectRevert();\n        market.liquidate(alice, 7_000e6, expected, bob);\n        vm.stopPrank();\n    }\n\n    function test_liquidationNeverLeavesDustDebt() public {\n        _deposit(alice, 10e18);\n        _borrow(alice, 14_000e6);\n        feed.setAnswer(1_600e8);\n\n        vm.startPrank(bob);\n        usdc.approve(address(market), type(uint256).max);\n        (uint256 repaid,) = market.liquidate(alice, type(uint256).max, 0, bob);\n        vm.stopPrank();\n\n        assertEq(repaid, 7_000e6);\n        uint256 remaining = market.debtOf(alice);\n        assertTrue(remaining == 0 || remaining >= market.minDebt());\n    }\n\n    function test_repayAmountIsAHardCeiling() public {\n        _deposit(alice, 10e18);\n        _borrow(alice, 14_000e6);\n        // Cv = $14,500 < debt * 1.05, so the whole position must be closed at once.\n        feed.setAnswer(1_450e8);\n\n        (uint256 required,) = market.previewLiquidation(alice, 1_000e6);\n        assertEq(required, 14_000e6);\n\n        vm.startPrank(bob);\n        usdc.approve(address(market), type(uint256).max);\n        // Asking to repay only $1,000 must revert rather than pull the full $14,000.\n        vm.expectRevert(abi.encodeWithSelector(LendingMarket.FullCloseRequired.selector, 14_000e6));\n        market.liquidate(alice, 1_000e6, 0, bob);\n        vm.stopPrank();\n\n        assertEq(usdc.balanceOf(bob), 1_000_000e6); // nothing pulled\n    }\n\n    function test_writeOffBadDebt() public {\n        _deposit(alice, 10e18);\n        _borrow(alice, 14_000e6);\n        feed.setAnswer(1_300e8);\n\n        vm.startPrank(bob);\n        usdc.approve(address(market), type(uint256).max);\n        market.liquidate(alice, type(uint256).max, 0, bob);\n        vm.stopPrank();\n\n        assertGt(market.debtOf(alice), 0);\n        vm.prank(owner);\n        market.writeOffBadDebt(alice);\n        assertEq(market.debtOf(alice), 0);\n        assertEq(market.totalDebtShares(), 0);\n    }\n\n    /* -------------------------------- oracle ----------------------------- */\n\n    function test_stalePriceReverts() public {\n        vm.warp(block.timestamp + 4000);\n        vm.expectRevert();\n        oracle.price();\n    }\n\n    function test_nonPositiveAnswerReverts() public {\n        feed.setAnswer(0);\n        vm.expectRevert(ChainlinkPriceOracle.NonPositiveAnswer.selector);\n        oracle.price();\n    }\n\n    function test_outOfBandAnswerReverts() public {\n        feed.setAnswer(50e8); // below the $100 floor\n        vm.expectRevert(abi.encodeWithSelector(ChainlinkPriceOracle.PriceOutOfBounds.selector, 50e8));\n        oracle.price();\n    }\n\n    function test_incompleteRoundReverts() public {\n        feed.setAnsweredInRound(0);\n        vm.expectRevert(ChainlinkPriceOracle.StaleRound.selector);\n        oracle.price();\n    }\n\n    /* ----------------------------- access control ------------------------ */\n\n    function test_onlyOwnerCanTouchParameters() public {\n        vm.startPrank(alice);\n        vm.expectRevert();\n        market.setBorrowRateBps(100);\n        vm.expectRevert();\n        market.pause();\n        vm.expectRevert();\n        market.withdrawSupply(1e6, alice);\n        vm.expectRevert();\n        market.writeOffBadDebt(bob);\n        vm.stopPrank();\n    }\n\n    function test_rateIsCapped() public {\n        vm.prank(owner);\n        vm.expectRevert(LendingMarket.InvalidParameters.selector);\n        market.setBorrowRateBps(5_001);\n    }\n\n    function test_ownerCannotSweepUserAssets() public {\n        vm.startPrank(owner);\n        vm.expectRevert(LendingMarket.InvalidParameters.selector);\n        market.sweep(address(weth), owner, 1);\n        vm.expectRevert(LendingMarket.InvalidParameters.selector);\n        market.sweep(address(usdc), owner, 1);\n        vm.stopPrank();\n    }\n\n    function test_ownerCannotWithdrawCollateralViaSupplyWithdrawal() public {\n        _deposit(alice, 10e18);\n        // withdrawSupply is bounded by the USDC balance and never touches WETH.\n        vm.prank(owner);\n        market.withdrawSupply(1_000_000e6, owner);\n        assertEq(weth.balanceOf(address(market)), 10e18);\n        assertEq(market.collateralOf(alice), 10e18);\n    }\n\n    /* -------------------------------- fuzz ------------------------------- */\n\n    /// @dev Solvency invariant: a borrower can never walk away with more debt-token value\n    ///      than their collateral was worth at the max LTV.\n    function testFuzz_cannotBorrowBeyondLtv(uint256 collateral, uint256 borrowAmount) public {\n        collateral = bound(collateral, 1e15, 100e18);\n        uint256 limit = collateral * START_PRICE / 1e20 * 7_000 / 10_000;\n        vm.assume(limit >= 500e6);\n        borrowAmount = bound(borrowAmount, 500e6, limit + 1e6);\n\n        _deposit(alice, collateral);\n        vm.prank(alice);\n        if (borrowAmount > limit) {\n            vm.expectRevert();\n            market.borrow(borrowAmount, alice);\n        } else {\n            market.borrow(borrowAmount, alice);\n            assertGe(market.healthFactor(alice), 1e18);\n        }\n    }\n\n    /// @dev While a position is still solvent (collateral worth at least the debt), a\n    ///      liquidation must never make it worse: either health improves, or the position is\n    ///      closed out entirely. This is the property the full-close band exists to preserve.\n    function testFuzz_solventLiquidationNeverDegradesPosition(uint256 newPrice, uint256 repayAmount) public {\n        _deposit(alice, 10e18);\n        _borrow(alice, 14_000e6);\n\n        // Cv >= $14,000 == debt, so the position is unhealthy but not yet insolvent.\n        newPrice = bound(newPrice, 1_400e8, 1_640e8);\n        feed.setAnswer(int256(newPrice));\n        vm.assume(market.healthFactor(alice) < 1e18);\n\n        repayAmount = bound(repayAmount, 1e6, 14_000e6);\n        (uint256 previewRepaid,) = market.previewLiquidation(alice, repayAmount);\n        vm.assume(previewRepaid > 0);\n\n        uint256 hfBefore = market.healthFactor(alice);\n        vm.startPrank(bob);\n        usdc.approve(address(market), type(uint256).max);\n        market.liquidate(alice, repayAmount, 0, bob);\n        vm.stopPrank();\n\n        assertTrue(market.healthFactor(alice) >= hfBefore || market.collateralOf(alice) == 0);\n        // A solvent position must never be turned into bad debt.\n        assertTrue(market.debtOf(alice) == 0 || market.collateralOf(alice) > 0);\n    }\n\n    /// @dev Once a position is genuinely insolvent, paying any bonus at all necessarily grows\n    ///      the shortfall — no liquidation rule avoids that. What must still hold is that\n    ///      every liquidation makes strict progress and that `previewLiquidation` is exact.\n    function testFuzz_insolventLiquidationMakesProgress(uint256 newPrice, uint256 repayAmount) public {\n        _deposit(alice, 10e18);\n        _borrow(alice, 14_000e6);\n\n        // Cv < $14,000 == debt: underwater.\n        newPrice = bound(newPrice, 900e8, 1_399e8);\n        feed.setAnswer(int256(newPrice));\n\n        repayAmount = bound(repayAmount, 1e6, 14_000e6);\n        (uint256 previewRepaid, uint256 previewSeized) = market.previewLiquidation(alice, repayAmount);\n        vm.assume(previewRepaid > 0);\n\n        uint256 debtBefore = market.debtOf(alice);\n        uint256 collateralBefore = market.collateralOf(alice);\n\n        vm.startPrank(bob);\n        usdc.approve(address(market), type(uint256).max);\n        (uint256 repaid, uint256 seized) = market.liquidate(alice, repayAmount, 0, bob);\n        vm.stopPrank();\n\n        assertEq(repaid, previewRepaid);\n        assertEq(seized, previewSeized);\n        assertLt(market.debtOf(alice), debtBefore);\n        assertLt(market.collateralOf(alice), collateralBefore);\n        assertEq(weth.balanceOf(bob), seized);\n    }\n\n    /// @dev Repeated liquidations must converge: a position can never be ground down\n    ///      forever while staying liquidatable.\n    function test_liquidationConvergesInTheThinBand() public {\n        _deposit(alice, 10e18);\n        _borrow(alice, 14_000e6);\n\n        // Cv = $14,500, debt = $14,000. Cv < debt * 1.05, so a capped 50% liquidation\n        // would make health *worse*; the full-close rule must kick in instead.\n        feed.setAnswer(1_450e8);\n        assertLt(market.healthFactor(alice), 1e18);\n\n        vm.startPrank(bob);\n        usdc.approve(address(market), type(uint256).max);\n        (uint256 repaid,) = market.liquidate(alice, type(uint256).max, 0, bob);\n        vm.stopPrank();\n\n        assertEq(repaid, 14_000e6); // whole debt, not the 50% close factor\n        assertEq(market.debtOf(alice), 0); // no bad debt manufactured\n        // All 10 WETH ($14,500) goes to the liquidator for a $14,000 repayment: a 3.6%\n        // bonus rather than the headline 5%, because that is all the collateral supports.\n        assertEq(weth.balanceOf(bob), 10e18);\n        assertEq(market.collateralOf(alice), 0);\n    }\n}\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":454,"oldLines":6,"newStart":454,"newLines":8,"lines":["         repayAmount = bound(repayAmount, 1e6, 14_000e6);","         (uint256 previewRepaid,) = market.previewLiquidation(alice, repayAmount);","         vm.assume(previewRepaid > 0);","+        // Skip the cases the forced close-out band rejects outright; they pull nothing.","+        vm.assume(previewRepaid <= repayAmount);"," ","         uint256 hfBefore = market.healthFactor(alice);","         vm.startPrank(bob);"]}],"userModified":false,"replaceAll":false}}

newPrice = bound(newPrice, 900e8, 1_399e8);\n        feed.setAnswer(int256(newPrice));\n\n        (uint256 previewRepaid, uint256 previewSeized) = market.previewLiquidation(alice,
